# Wrok Bot 后端实施台账

更新时间：2026-10-02。用户明确恢复实施，仅复核补全 v7/R422 对已合并交付的要求。074/075/076/077分别由PR #95/#96/#97/#98合入，入场main为`8fabc9f634c2f8c640373bf96d2bca8b80559726`。原PR、历史验收及未完成项保持；本轮新证据另列，不启动全新未交付能力。

001–047 已核实为 main 祖先。换机主控已亲读、实测并正常合入049、050、051、052、053、056、057、058及重建060/061、新增必要维护063/064；055、072、073也已按PR及main祖先事实核实合入。048、054已于2026-10-12关闭未合入（ARCHIVE-20261012-01），059/062未完成，不能继续用旧待批准记录替代当前合同及实际检查状态。后端规范原件已在本机核对；旧机原始 QA 和其余缺失输入尚未恢复，不能把历史自报转记为新机验收通过。下方历次检查点是历史记录，当前状态以任务表及本轮最终结论为准。

本台账记录实施与验证事实，不定义产品能力或架构。每个任务对应一个独立 PR，按冻结合同及实际依赖顺序集成。PR 链接中的合并状态与提交是远端集成事实；局部测试通过不表示产品阶段或发布验收完成。

接续记录：065旧候选`0fee263c`严格拒绝四类记录的72个畸形输入；完整侧车回归102通过、1失败、0忽略，失败为拒绝损坏consumed时已替换既有epoch。主控复核原始授权覆盖必要返修，已在同一owner的回收闭包先核consumed，再持久mint epoch，再删启动锁；没有新增权限或错误码。返修`077bb653`模块15/15通过，完整侧车109通过、1失败、0忽略，失败为既有审批夹具时间倒置；该独立缺陷由070修复并合入，最终065含台账组合须重新实测。历史失败与原断言保留；坏consumed时prior epoch字节/inode保持是本任务回归属性，不冒充规范逐字要求。067候选`ddfd7831`模块16/16、完整侧车104/104（含真实PG）、runtime/launcher严格Clippy及格式检查通过；该旧main候选不替代依赖整合后验收，亦不计作当前main同候选A门证据。

069候选`add48717`定向12/12、runtime/launcher all-targets严格Clippy与格式检查通过。该输入面沿用现有UTF-8、trim及权限/硬链接语义；第一次固定17字节探测，成功后的字节重核仍有界，整次最多33字节。私有helper及三层错误映射已实测，拒绝后真实epoch/consumed/required/applied、helper journal、动态锁和自有data/WAL字节及inode保持。打开后增长证据由原语诊断及生产私有helper测试共同支持，不声称穷举任意生产调度竞态；最终组合回归及最后候选以本任务PR记录为准。069依赖已合015/R296，不依赖065–068代码；其完成也不关闭A1、M0或A0–A7。

070修复既有真实PG组合测试中两条INSERT分别多次读取时钟导致的时间倒置；在065修复候选完整回归中实际观察到请求时间比创建时间早4微秒，触发既有约束。两条测试INSERT改用各自同一语句时间，保留30分钟未来到期值及授权失效后的取消、过期和审计断言，生产代码与DDL不改。main基线定向1/1、代码候选`39ae2620`定向1/1均实际通过，基线通过反映该缺陷的间歇性，未改写为失败；最终同候选检查及集成以本任务PR为准。此局部夹具修复不代表065完整验收、自然TTL边界或M0通过。

071仅调整既有取消测试的观察watchdog：完整start先串行完成三个version helper，Fresh路径再initdb，原300×10ms局部预算可能在主startup child出现前耗尽。预算由现有version时限乘3、initdb时限及readiness时限相加形成当前55秒，仍须读到真实child_observed，提前返回真实Join/result并失败，原取消、证据保存及第二次启动断言保持。主控受控2秒helper输入在原窗口0/1，仅watchdog改变后1/1；main代码候选`820c11d6`定向1/1。该因果对照不唯一解释原间歇full失败；55秒只是测试watchdog，不能抢占同步IO，不构成启动SLA。两条正式夹具仍用原0.2秒，产品时限和恢复行为不变，未关闭065–068组合门禁、A1或M0。最终含台账的同候选完整检查与集成以本任务PR实际记录为准。

066沿用四类恢复记录既有1..=512字节规则，用已打开句柄的513字节首次探测执行读取预算，保留原有权限、硬链接、I/O错误映射及定位后重核；065的consumed→mint→删锁顺序和071正式测试不变。旧私有组合`3f22e74`模块21/21、完整真实PG116/116、双严格Clippy/fmt已验；当前接已验065main的新候选须独立最终检查，旧结果不冒充新候选通过。空、512、513、打开后增长、四类canonical及坏格式拒绝现场均有局部见证；512字节只表示原语长度接受，生产解析仍校验闭格式。该输入面不证明任意竞态调度、递归data/WAL完整性、A1或M0。

## macOS 首发进度

当前尚无 A0–A7 中任何一项取得完整同候选通过证据；局部 PR 数量不代表首发完成比例。持续实施到首发验收完成。

| 部分 | 当前事实 |
|---|---|
| 启动与数据保护 | 多个真实 PG 子场景已验；完整签名 App 旅程仍待验 |
| 三模型 | custom 后端与四入口UI已局部验收；SDK5及账户身份已合，PG/Vault已完成本机及组合验收，集成记录见047；登录及模型运行组合仍待，账户桥Rust适配待做 |
| Browser / 原生 | 有协调核心；实际执行、画面与GUI完整装配仍有缺口 |
| 备份恢复 | 加密/归档基础已验；PG恢复、切换及完整演练未完成 |
| 签名与交付 | 旧机曾记录有效 Developer ID 身份；本轮新机查询为 0 个有效身份，实际候选签名、公证及发行图验收待完成 |

## 历史验证（原始 QA 本轮未取回）

040 主控已亲读实现与测试，并在同一候选运行：

- `cargo test -p openbot-infra --offline --locked --lib -- backup::archive_bundle`：22 通过，包括 13 项解包测试和 9 项容器回归。
- `cargo test -p openbot-domain --offline --locked --lib -- backup::recovery_`：9 通过。
- `python3 -m unittest tools.test_repository_guard`：8 通过。
- `cargo check -p openbot-infra --offline --locked --no-default-features --features desktop-local-vault --lib`：通过。

认证后的总长度不符、有效前缀后的外包末块、损坏和乱序均无成功结果。未执行 PostgreSQL 恢复或实际 OS 验收。

041 主控已亲读实现与独立预期字节测试，归档链 29 项通过（7 项写出边界、13 项解包、9 项容器回归），Desktop Local infra 的 offline/locked 构建通过。写出字段顺序与原格式一致，超预算时输出 writer 的 write/flush 调用数均为零；实际 I/O 失败仍可保留已写前缀。

042 主控已验证 domain 恢复 13 项、归档链 30 项以及 Desktop Local infra 构建通过。独立探针用相同 4096 字节输入确认信封为 8334 字节，解析结果由失败变为成功；最大合法数据经归档写读和认证解包恢复原字节。

043 主控亲读六个源码/测试文件，使用独立 PostgreSQL 17.11 验证：已知账本前缀拒绝矩阵、0032形态回归、Desktop bootstrap错误master/canary改删/跨库proof矩阵、实际sidecar→Vault→Application组合，四个定向测试均通过；Desktop Launcher all-target offline/locked check通过。当前32无重复迁移；真实32→下一新schema的升级矩阵随首次新migration另验。

044 主控核定 exact SDK5 制品/源码/LICENSE，Cargo.lock仅SDK版本/checksum变化；三个依赖许可原文及SPDX关系核同，原59条来源保留、总62条。18项真实TLS测试、六目标依赖图、四组Server/SSO/Desktop feature并集、Launcher all-target check均通过，SDK自带HTTP/TLS/WS和UI依赖边均未启用。初始测试模块路径和HTTP EOF预期错误已修正留证；SDK `[DONE]`后的body Drop保留Cancelled事实，不伪报HTTP EOF或用户取消。

045 主控亲读九个源码/测试/守卫文件；真实 TLS 测试 24 项通过（原 18 项与新 6 项），依赖边界检查及 Desktop Launcher all-target offline/locked check 通过。验证显式开放的账户端点、固定身份协议、无效请求在发送前拒绝、身份一致性、脱敏、取消、零重定向及 64 KiB 路由预算。结果仅为协议读取能力，未证明真实账户登录、凭据持久化或完整三模型旅程。

046 主控亲读目录迁移及全部内容变化，最终候选 216 项 UI 单测、9 项发布守卫、严格 Clippy、生产 WASM/release 构建、中英文 1068 键、样式与资源预算检查通过。模型四入口、FIFO、有序技能、键盘、明确冲突和 Unknown 共 13 项浏览器请求场景在队列修复候选通过；其后错误提示修复重验 5 项受影响场景，最后收件人恢复修复在最终构建重验。不同构建的证据分别保留，未冒充全部场景在最终构建重跑。创建响应丢失时不再次创建，运行结果不明时仅显式原请求重试；目录版本冲突要求重新选择。首次读回操作漏选模型的失败记录保留并按原预期重做。浏览器使用合成 HTTP/SSE 后端；实际厂商、PG组合、Wry、完整可访问性与首发 A 门仍待。CSS 为 130505/131072 字节，已超过预警线，未放宽预算。UI 依赖守卫使用 locked/offline 元数据选中的实际来源通过；默认全局缓存因重复 registry 源首次拒绝，失败保留，未修改全局缓存或依赖。

047 主控亲读 25 个产品、schema、测试和守卫文件。独立 PostgreSQL 验证：历史及新增 schema 6 项、Desktop bootstrap 3 项、Server 初始化 4 项、人员撤权恢复 1 项、自定义模型三协议 PG/TLS 1 项均通过。SDK 持久授权 12 个场景分两次完成验证（首轮 11 通过，纠正 SDK Missing 对象语义的测试预期后，剩余 1 项通过）；原 24 项 TLS、78 项数据库单测、依赖守卫和 Launcher all-target check 通过。初期编译错误和失败日志已保留；四个越界格式改动已恢复。并发刷新仅一次请求，响应丢失、取消、主体漂移及两阶段审计故障后保留未决状态，不重发旧令牌。接入已合入的 046 后，25 个后端文件及 265 个 UI/路径文件的已验内容均不变；主控补跑 Launcher all-target、SDK 依赖守卫及 9 项发布守卫通过。真实 App 登录、v2 模型运行和厂商旅程仍待。

052 主控亲读 Desktop/Server 双侧装配代码及第一真源 §8.1、§28.1 历史修订条目，确认 Server 已用 `ScreenHub`+`ScreenSessionService` 装配 `screen_sessions` 端口，Desktop 仍是 fail-closed 的 `NoScreenSessionAdministration` 占位；将 Desktop 对齐到 Server 已验证的同一模式，范围严格限定于生产装配收敛，不改动端口 trait 或 Computer 侧实现。`cargo check`/`cargo clippy --no-deps -D warnings` 在 `desktop-local-runtime` 与更完整的 `desktop-launcher` 两个 feature 集下均与未改动的 `origin/main` 逐行 diff 为空；针对本机真实 PostgreSQL 17 的 `--ignored` 集成测试新增 `IssueScreenSession` 断言，证明端口现在对目标可见性做真实判定（返回 `AppError::NotVisible`）而非旧 stub 恒定的 `DependencyUnavailable`；该测试与全量非 ignored 套件（341 通过）均通过。验证中发现的两处既有问题（`openbot-desktop` 自身第三波 Clippy 红、4 个 PostgreSQL sidecar 失败路径测试在本沙箱确定性失败）已通过 `git stash` A/B 确认与本次改动无关，归档为 #65、#66，未在本 PR 修复。本次改动不启动任何 engine 进程、不构造 `HostLocalBrowserRuntime`，也不涉及 Tool/Policy/Agent 层对 `BrowserOperation` 的执行管线；C05 的完整 Browser 产品链仍待后续多个 PR 完成。

051 历史候选 bfb79b542b02ed470cafa3bbecceccb855cf5859 的作者记录（原始 QA 本轮未取回；新增 allow 已进入返修）：

051 主控亲读 gateway_authority 三个测试文件与 gateway_sdk_transport/tls.rs，修复 issue #61 记录的第二波既有 clippy 问题（此前被 #58 的库编译失败长期遮蔽）：10 处（原报告 9 处，复核时另发现 1 处未覆盖的同构代码）`.err().expect(msg)`，其中 9 处机械改为等价的 `.expect_err(msg)`；第 10 处（`pending_failures.rs` 的 `assert_pending_without_second_post`）因 `Ok` 类型 `acosmi::Client` 有意不实现 `Debug`（避免意外泄漏存活 token）导致该改法无法编译，保留原写法并加定点 `#[allow(clippy::err_expect)]` 及理由注释。`gateway_sdk_transport/tls.rs` 的 3 处 `dead_code`（`Capture::headers`、`TlsFixture::tls_failures`、`chat_text`）根因是该文件被 `gateway_authority.rs` 与 `gateway_sdk_transport.rs` 两个测试二进制通过 `include!` 共享，字段/函数在后者的编译单元里有真实读取、在前者里没有；加 `#[allow(dead_code)]` 及理由注释，未做删除（删除会破坏真正用到它们的二进制）。验证：`cargo clippy -p openbot-infra --all-targets -- -D warnings` 干净；`gateway_authority`（12 项）与 `gateway_sdk_transport`（24 项）在真实 PostgreSQL 17 下 `--include-ignored` 全通过；lib 370 项不受影响。核实全工作区 `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets` 目前仍会在 `openbot-testkit` 的 `transport_parity.rs` 处以硬编译错误（E0004，非 clippy 告警）终止，与本次改动无关，系初始快照自带的既有缺口（已用 git stash 在无关干净副本上单独复现确认），已单独归档为 issue #63，未在本 PR 修复范围内。
050 修复 issue #57 记录的既有失败：native_0027.rs / native_0028.rs 各自的幂等重检误用裸 native::apply()（目标随 NATIVE_LATEST_VERSION 漂移），改为 native_0029/native_0033 已确立的 apply_through(client, 自身版本) 写法，未改动任何产品代码。真实 PostgreSQL 17.11 验证：`cargo test -p openbot-infra --test native_0027 --test native_0028 --offline --locked -- --include-ignored` 两项转绿；全 crate `--no-fail-fast -- --include-ignored` 除一项既有、自诊断、与本次无关的环境性失败（schema_baseline_parity.rs 因本机 pg_hba 为 trust 认证无法验证口令是否泄漏，需 scram/md5 实例）外全部通过；两个改动文件的 clippy 干净。

049 修复 archive_bundle.rs::hex_decode 中 `value.len() % 2 != 0` 触发的 clippy::manual_is_multiple_of（issue #58）。该 lint 在当前 rustc/clippy 1.98.0 下会使严格门禁 `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings` 对任何 PR 必现失败，属既有代码、与具体改动无关。改为 `!value.len().is_multiple_of(2)`，语义等价，无行为变化。`cargo clippy -p openbot-infra --lib --offline --locked -- -D warnings` 转绿；`cargo test -p openbot-infra --lib --offline --locked` 370 项通过。修复后补跑全量 `--workspace --all-targets`，发现被该 lib 编译失败长期掩盖的第二波既有红（gateway_authority 测试 9 处 err_expect、gateway_sdk_transport/tls.rs 3 处 dead_code），已登记为 issue #61，作为独立后续任务处理，未在本次改动。

058 Copilot（临时实施执行方，非主控亲验）完成实现与本机验证，待主控独立验收：在完全干净的 `origin/main`（`66274f8`）上逐 crate 补跑测试时，发现 `openbot-agent` 的 `test_official_fixture_provenance_integrity` 既有失败（issue #75，与本次改动前任何工作无关）。根因是 `fixtures/agui/official-event-family.provenance.json` 对 `README.md` 记录的 `bytes`/`sha256` 是过期值（1341 字节），而该文件自本仓库当前历史根提交 `15d66ee` 起实际一直是 231 字节；`git log --oneline --all` 溯源确认 1341 字节仅存在于一个不在 `origin/main` 祖先链上的悬空分支草稿，从未对应过任何真正入库的内容。README.md 是 `vendored_schema` 里唯一标注"OpenBot-authored documentation"（非上游 vendor）的条目，故以当前实际、长期未变的文件内容为准，只更新 `bytes`/`sha256` 两个字段，未碰 `source` 字符串（属 #59/V6-PR-048 命名任务范围）、README.md 正文或其余 6 个已核对无误的 vendor 文件条目。`cargo test -p openbot-agent --all-targets`：58 项 lib 测试 + 5 项 fixture 测试全部通过（此前 4 通过/1 失败）；`cargo clippy -p openbot-agent --all-targets -- -D warnings` 干净。改动范围仅 provenance.json 的 2 行。

## V6-PR-073 Unix诊断fixture codesign输出有界保留

2026-10-02：从正常已合主线 `b62f3ae4e84d8998703113ab62363d5d3eb59c0c` 维护既有 `verify_fixture_signing` 的两次诊断捕获。metadata只保留stderr最多64KiB+1，entitlements只保留stdout最多16KiB+1，额外1字节仅标记原预算超限；另一流零保留并持续排空。正常与超限路径均读完两流真实EOF后wait原持有Child，不增总输出限制或deadline。状态失败/原预算超限仍先于UTF-8、flags和plist解析；恰好64KiB/16KiB仍接受。已有锁定rustix1.1.4仅在testkit原optional、xtask依赖边追加event；workspace依赖声明、版本、checksum及Cargo.lock不变，不增加生产依赖。

读取控制流按Rust1.98实际实现保持：stdout优先poll、Interrupted重试、任一EOF后剩流恢复blocking读取。HUP/ERR/NVAL进入read，事件本身不充当EOF；blocking剩流的WouldBlock保留hard-read错误。不可恢复读/模式/poll错误仍经原式unwrap在wait前panic、关闭pipe且不保证等待Child或排空另一流；此历史生命周期缺口未修复，不把正常/超限的wait保证扩写到读故障。没有新增线程错误路径。非Unix保留原捕获实现；本项运行证据仅macOS/Unix。

干净检查候选 `019c2889fdfd27a85afd2a2db285211ea2d97429`（tree `73b8108268413a9bd69c85f47ffb67f2c7f8b634`）在固定Rust1.98.0、独立target、offline/locked及既有原生构建依赖路径下，实际运行 `openbot-testkit --features xtask --bin xtask engine_bundle::tests`：16 passed、0 failed、0 ignored、0 measured、179 filtered；11项新增覆盖边界/增长、零保留、读故障、两流各2MiB的四种组合、buffered HUP、EOF后原Child等待和错误优先级。同feature all-targets严格Clippy（`-D warnings`）及workspace fmt check均退出0。原首轮缺xmlsec1-config的构建101/零测试与新增测试helper的Clippy101均保留；后者已不放宽lint地修复，旧9fea候选15/15只作该候选历史。

最终登记只改台账，并把072任务行校正为实际PR92/b62f3ae合入事实；原055、072、065–068记录及失败历史不改写。完整tracked树除台账与上述检查候选逐字节一致，精确引用该候选真实局部回执，不冒充在登记后HEAD重新执行。未运行真实codesign、签名证书、PG、TTY、Keychain或原生权限探测；签名profile、entitlements、命令、manifest、生产Engine协议、权限及release拒绝路径保持。本项不是生产Engine合同冻结、真实签名验收或A0–A7/M0完成。

## V6-PR-076 原run终态后的tool journal写入防护

2026-10-02：从已合主线 `36cfd67f284103d26b54afe6547e011993003d24` 为首次decision、retry、capability绑定和低层outcome四个公共Repo写入口增加原run防护。事务显式Read Committed，先锁原run，只有running可写；run锁等待上限5秒，后续call/attempt使用NOWAIT。普通journal outcome逐项核对原decision、receipt、actor/Bot、capability与持久metadata，并使用receipt所指attempt的实际序号。合法竞争拒绝与持久损坏分类分开，过期或不匹配审批在decision边界返回Conflict；outcome和audit同事务，普通audit故障返回Unavailable并回滚。

remember保留原actor优先的强guard、取得锁后的独立receipt读取和错误语义；低层outcome拒绝remember，草稿改名不能绕过。没有新增执行重试、成功写入重放、权限或解除Unknown占用。原terminal后的refusal追加audit，以及R322认证代次推进对executing attempt的既有处理保持；后者本批仅核源码未变，不冒称重新执行Desktop启动旅程。

固定Rust1.98.0、offline/locked、`openbot-infra --no-default-features --features server-runtime`、`CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0`的实际检查通过411个不同用例，零failed/ignored：Infra lib360；真实PG新journal fence18、新Application/Runtime旅程6、既有remember16、run runtime5、tool application5、Repo精确回归1。新旅程通过真实Application、PostgresToolJournal和PostgresRunRuntime，覆盖三处暂停与四terminal、旧写入拒绝、新合法lease不受影响、精确历史回放零mutation及RR在新Runtime下仍阻止begin；真实BuiltIn worker在第一次effect后的outcome失败时自然退出，不执行第二tool或继续sampling。executor效果为合成计数，不代表外部vendor验收。

锁竞争用真实入口、真实terminal事务和`pg_blocking_pids`等待链证明先后顺序，包含默认RR连接上的新RC读取、writer先提交/回滚、run超时和NOWAIT失败。六个具名23505分类使用隔离测试表上的明确故障trigger，真实重复写另有用例；未知状态、NULL/断链及audit invariant只在专属一次性数据库注入损坏，不能作为原schema的正常旅程。正向、审批、竞争和Application旅程各用完整迁移的新库，生产SQL、迁移及既有fixture未改。

受影响lib及两个新测试目标的严格Clippy、全仓fmt和diff检查通过。首次Clippy因测试枚举共享前缀失败，原记录保留；仅更名三份旅程文件内的私有变体后重跑严格检查及6项旅程通过，不放宽lint。每次执行保存完整输入摘要；其余45项PG与360项lib只按逐项未变的实际编译输入引用，排除的三个独立journey测试文件均有明确差量记录，不冒称最终HEAD全部重跑。三次自有PG均实际stop等待退出0，未使用用户数据库、备份、Keychain或TTY。

最终源码候选 `e6435ece9051d230775e5bc88856fa84f8b35dc7`（tree `54c67523703ad2cfc923fccb37f86fd53ccd06c9`，base `36cfd67f284103d26b54afe6547e011993003d24`）已完成独立实施与原始证据复核，7项最终记录、411个不同通过用例及28项局部验收映射核同，无阻断项。1125个产品/构建输入与已验候选逐项相同；本次后续登记只更新公开台账，不重复运行未变化的检查。最终HEAD及实际集成见[PR #97](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/97)。

## V6-PR-077 兼容foreground占用投影

2026-10-02：从已合主线 `9f4f70d79746e43b25d4dd9a403dde3131227d85` 按R409增加native0036。迁移在显式Read Committed事务中锁定runs，以精确thread/run复合外键建立两列占用投影，完整回填并核验；重复apply只核验、不修复。AFTER run trigger维护自身占用，正常完成/失败/取消仅释放精确槽；Unknown仍持有槽。原run身份、foreground、RR状态、终态重新激活及直接投影写入有防护，删除/cascade/TRUNCATE不能绕过。保留旧partial unique index，未提供处置、解除Unknown、重试或新权限。

begin、conversation、RunRepo active及074/075两个只读页共用同一静态完整性谓词，在各自SQL快照内同时检查缺失和反向错绑，不回退到历史status。已可见但坏投影拒绝；新begin先核原目标权限，再核投影，其他runtime的有效lease不能掩盖损坏；历史精确重放保持原授权语义并核投影。074/075当前owner、认证代次和完整ACL保持，conversation仍用原scope/membership合同。begin显式RC及5秒事务锁等待；维护trigger的5秒设置仅约束函数内等待，自身槽用NOWAIT，不声称覆盖进入AFTER前的run或旧索引等待。

固定Rust1.98.0、offline/locked、`CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0`，最终完整输入未变的定向检查通过465个不同Rust用例，零failed/ignored：Infra lib360；实际PG上的0031/0035/0036迁移13、占用11、导入4、074/075查询14、remember17、Repo5、run runtime5、begin6、conversation1、tool application5、076 journal fence18及journey6。真实数据库覆盖全部六状态/foreground组合、旧writer锁屏障及默认RR下的RC迁移、fresh/begin实际隔离观察、回滚、重复apply坏槽、精确释放、多行失败整体回滚、锁冲突、导入RR和DO NOTHING零投影写入、五消费者双向损坏及权限优先级。新增实际PG重启旅程：真实remember产生正向effect/receipt，journal失败后真实Runtime进入RR；控制器等待PG停止、核PID消失、同data-dir启动，Rust核postmaster启动时间变新。重建生产adapter后五消费者、exact replay及历史remember回读保持原effect/非空executing attempt/terminal/占用，新begin仍被占用拒绝；原lease已过期，不能冒充阻塞原因。0036 fixture由新建隔离PG实际提取，生成操作不计产品测试。

### V7-IMPL-006 finite publication wording clarification

Unsupported applies to unimplemented or missing producers; observed ProviderUnknown and unproven configuration keep their own Unavailable state and reason. The final joint identity/source SQL statement uses one Read Committed statement snapshot; prechecks, separate collectors, postchecks and the final request witness are not one shared database snapshot. The five-second monotonic budget covers the Application query and its final decision; HTTP response transmission lifetime was not measured as that deadline. These precise qualifications supersede the broader draft wording above. Product inputs, executed tests, original source identities and the finite acceptance scope are unchanged.

### V7-IMPL-028 — Server authorization journal lifecycle

Product candidate: `46eae75aca1d65d266574bc171a8c98f2cf23c7d`. Independent ACTUAL acceptance of this finite task passed and was adopted. Publication, admin merge and delivery remain pending.

Connects the authorization journal to the real Server session and single-user Hosts. After verified gateway metadata is read, a current Host can create an attempt, retain its whole captured registration request, admit registration using a full-row compare-and-swap, or close the original created/admitted attempt for a refusal or an unknown dependency outcome. Each write includes its typed audit event in the same transaction and requires the original commit acknowledgement and exact readback. Late and unknown acknowledgements retain their classifications.

Startup binds one journal/runtime pair to the original Pool and deployment/tenant scope. Missing installation configuration leaves the journal unavailable; malformed configuration is rejected. Operations preserve current Host/actor, lifetime, cancellation and saved-budget checks. No migration or dependency is added.

- V7-IMPL-037: Products 367e382e245e37be9cf44eba5aa8c90cd9ab0d92 and review follow-up 9c2f331 close the 036 follow-up "Run-locked writer transactions now include the per-transaction schema observation". RC writer transactions now take the complete same-transaction schema observation once, before their first row lock, as a CurrentWriterSchema proof bound to that transaction; writer admission and the channel source consume it instead of observing again; record_from_message takes only the proof and runs on the proof's own transaction. Covered: Run chunk, remote projection, tool exchange, finish, rejected dispatch, unstarted cancellation, begin V1/V2 (one observation instead of two, or four on channel Threads, under the Thread and channel locks) and RepoCore typed body inserts; stale-run recovery observes after its SKIP LOCKED probe selects one lease-expired Run, so that expired Run, its lease and outbox rows stay locked across the observation while idle relay ticks pay nothing. No row lock, advisory lock, unique wait or NOWAIT/SKIP LOCKED mode was added, removed or reordered; the observation takes only AccessShareLock on catalogs and the migration ledger. SERIALIZABLE component and remote-interrupt transactions keep their original order, because an earlier first statement would age their snapshot. RunRuntime writers keep inheriting the server default isolation; production uses READ COMMITTED, and under an operator REPEATABLE READ default the older snapshot can turn a concurrent commit to the same Run/Thread/lease into a natural 40001, which keeps its Unavailable class with full rollback. Measured on owned PG17.11 from server statement logs of the real debug-build paths: Run/Thread/lease hold for chunk appends fell from a 118.5 ms to a 2.0 ms median and for finish from 141.5 ms to 4.0 ms; direct-Thread begin advisory/Thread hold fell from about 279 ms to about 10 ms and its whole transaction from about 280 ms to about 147 ms. A SQL replay of the finish sequence gave 32.6 ms to 2.3 ms server-side. The new owned-PG test writers_observe_current_schema_before_their_first_row_lock blocks the observation with an ACCESS EXCLUSIVE ledger holder and proves from the real waiting backend that chunk, finish and begin hold no Run/Thread/lease row or advisory lock while observing; it also covers a channel Thread (finish with assistant text through the channel source, begin on the existing channel Thread with the channel row probed), ties the waiter to the blocker with pg_blocking_pids, first proves the probe refuses a real Run/Thread/lease holder, and fails on the previous order. Strict Clippy passed for the infra library with desktop-local-vault, server-runtime and both; on the final head 18 owned-PG targets passed 139 of 144 cases (including SERIALIZABLE component decisions, maintenance import and custom-model V2 error mapping); the five failures that reproduce identically on main or need the owned restart controller or PG binary directory (run_cancellation and thread_conversation report thread_target_not_visible on main as well). Server-SSO builds were not compiled on this host because xmlsec is not installed. An independent read-only review found no P1; its follow-ups are applied, and maintenance import still observes twice outside any Run lock. Existing reverse orders outside this change (cancel producer Run then outbox against outbox-first consumers, stale recovery against begin, custom start Thread/Run/lease SHARE against lock_run) stay as recorded; artifact save and remote interrupt keep NOWAIT.

- V7-IMPL-038: Adds the trusted internal one-Thread stop/clear intent producer to the existing `PostgresThreadHistoryAuthority` (`close_for_stop_clear_before`), which is ACK1 only. In one original guarded READ COMMITTED read-write transaction on the same real current Server Host as the 034 observer, it takes the complete writer schema proof before its first row lock, repeats the full current single-Thread management check (current creator, live user/admin role, deny, generation, original Session or verified SingleUser owner, direct or channel access, live base), inserts one immutable `stop_clear` intent (database `clock_timestamp()` as its time, a fresh UUIDv7 operation id, the caller's canonical lowercase UUIDv7 request id, the actor and the Auth generation) and moves the matching admission head from open to closed from that very row, then settles the three deferred head/intent constraints as ordinary statements, runs a fresh current joint, and returns a typed result (non-Serde, non-Clone, no public constructor, redacted Debug) only after the genuine original COMMIT acknowledgement. An exact replay of the same request and actor repeats the current check and returns the original operation after the real ROLLBACK acknowledgement without writing; another request on a closed Thread, or a request id already used by another Thread, is a Conflict, classified by reading the stored rows rather than by swallowing a unique error; every refusal also returns only after its real ROLLBACK acknowledgement. Apart from the in-transaction schema observation, which can wait on a relation lock before any row is locked, the only row-lock and unique-key waits are the actor row (the existing 034 wait), the lazy head insert and the intent insert, each followed by a fresh current statement; the exclusive head lock and every positive current row lock are NOWAIT, and a refused or deadlocked lock is Unavailable, never closed and never Conflict (a deadlock is conceivable only on a pre-existing Thread that has no head row yet, and PostgreSQL's detection then surfaces as Unavailable). A Host failure after a known COMMIT is reported as that failure, never as Unknown, and keeps the stored intent, so the caller may re-issue the same request id for the exact replay; an unacknowledged COMMIT is Unknown and the same request id then replays. The owner's late-ACK class is mapped one to one to CommitAcknowledgedAfterDeadline, and so is a deadline exhausted right after a known COMMIT; neither can be triggered deterministically because the owner's timer decides first (an ACK withheld past the deadline is CommitUnknown), so both are untested residuals. The 034 observer is split mechanically into two private helpers with an identical statement sequence, locks and error mapping; no existing public signature changes. This does not cancel or drain in-flight Runs (they still settle through the existing closed-thread finish without body), delete history bytes, touch artifacts or quota, write audit, or grant ACK2, Ready or any public command, route, DTO or UI. No migration (native 0052 stays free), SQL, schema fixture, dependency or Contracts/Server/Desktop production change. Eight real Server Host cases passed on an owned loopback PostgreSQL 17.11 instance (the cloud environment's default instance is 17.10, which the pinned 033/035/036 fixtures reject, and was not used for the recorded runs): direct V1, V2 and private-channel close with exact intent/head rows, original facts unchanged and closed-writer refusals, including a Thread whose head does not exist yet; non-creator, later member, admin, LegacyUnknown, soft-deleted, hard-deleted and malformed-input refusals with nothing written, and a corrupt head/intent pair that is not repaired; exact replay and both Conflict forms with stale-generation and revoked-access rechecks; real lock waits and NOWAIT refusals observed on the server, with the schema proof shown to precede any row lock and the pair settled before COMMIT; an in-flight Run finishing on the closed path; withheld COMMIT and ROLLBACK acknowledgements (no result escapes) and a COMMIT acknowledgement withheld past the deadline or lost (Unknown, then resolved by replaying the same request id); a withdrawn Session, a Host closed while the transaction is still open (rolled back after the real ROLLBACK acknowledgement, nothing written) and a Host closed after a known COMMIT (Host, intent kept); and the verified SingleUser owner. The Desktop Local window Host case needs macOS and is pending verification there. Regression on the same instance: nine of the eleven 034 management cases and the 033, 035, 036 and 037 targets (12, 12, 12 and 1 cases) passed; the other two 034 cases use 250 ms and 300 ms original deadlines that the observer cannot meet on this cloud host, fail identically on the unchanged base and were not edited. ACK2, in-flight Run drain, byte deletion, restore consistency and Ready remain open. Accepted on the exact product head 5f6e3eb (tree 50cc2fab): the independent non-author review returned GO with no P1 (one review round added the open-transaction Host leg to the session-withdrawal case; the remaining notes are documentation-level); controller acceptance in a separate build directory on an owned PostgreSQL 17.11 instance found workspace formatting and five strict Clippy configurations clean, the eight cases passing, and the three concurrency cases passing ten repeated runs each (30/30). A directed sweep of 22 test binaries (the 033-037 thread history targets; thread begin, directory, live, conversation and history; run runtime, cancellation and occupancy; repositories; native 0016 and 0036; channel activity; component decisions; remote interrupt; intelligence import; approval-preference current host) matched unchanged main case by case, including main's existing failures (the two 034 cases with 250/300 ms deadlines, four approval-preference timing cases, and one run-cancellation and one conversation case reporting thread_target_not_visible). This entry records acceptance before merge; the merge is recorded separately.

- V7-IMPL-039: Consumer-side admission closure for a closed Thread history (the consumer half of ACK1). Every consumer that exists today now refuses to create anything new from a Thread whose history head is closed: the context load on the legacy and current-dataset routes (Stale, so the Run ends through the existing lease-lost reconciliation path with the stable code runtime_lease_expired and no provider is started), a custom-model start on both routes (the existing custom-model authority refusal, before the Vault is opened and before any network I/O), the first decision, a retry and a capability attach of the unified tool journal (the journal conflict), a new remember tool effect (conflict; an exact replay of an already committed receipt still returns its original receipt), the explicit save of a message text (a new operation and the start of IO read the source as unknown; an exact replay of a committed save still observes its original receipt, and a close between IO and registration follows the existing source-changed path that keeps the real byte observation and its charge, so nothing is published as available), and a user's memory made from a message of a closed Thread (unknown source; a memory with no source is unchanged). Tool outcomes, finish and terminal replays, every read, in-flight providers, tools and readers are not touched: nothing is stopped, drained, cleared or deleted, and no command, route, DTO, UI, audit event, error variant, failure code or migration is added (native 0052 stays unused). The read-write consumers (the tool journal writes, the remember effect, the explicit save and the manual memory) take the complete strict writer schema observation once, before their first row lock, and then the Thread and head share through the existing joint after their own permission, Run and lease locks; the share is held to the natural end of the transaction. The three transactions of the explicit save take the head in one order, after the actor, source, quota and operation rows. Both custom start routes only read the head, with one plain statement after their authority loads and before the Vault is opened, and keep no head lock across the send; the legacy route keeps no Thread lock either, so a durable cancel can still claim the Run while the response headers are pending. The current-dataset start already verifies the complete current writer schema once, in the lineage stage of its own authority load, so it takes no second observation (which would cost about 270 ms more in the unoptimized test profile). A close committed between that read and the send is left to the in-flight drain (ACK2) for every custom start route. The context load adds a lock-free closed-head predicate to its admission statement and checks it once more after the body read: a missing head reads as open, and a failed check is Unavailable, never open. Lock conflicts (55P03), deadlocks and query failures keep each entry's own Unavailable class and are never read as closed. Residual: the tool decision, retry and capability attach and the manual memory with a source message now take the Thread row FOR SHARE NOWAIT through the joint, which they did not lock before, and each transaction pays one complete writer-schema observation (a tool call at least two), so they can transiently return Unavailable while another transaction holds the Thread row, for example the event append of a streaming Run, a concurrent begin or a rename; this follows the existing NOWAIT discipline until a unified lock order exists, and no waiting was added. Eight new owned-PostgreSQL cases (tests/thread_history_consumer_closure.rs) close the head with test-owned privileged SQL, which gives no producer credit, and use real locks and pg_blocking_pids for every wait, never a sleep: the context load of both routes with a close committed behind the body read, the Run end without a provider start, the custom start on both routes (both shown to hold no head lock across the send, and the legacy start no Thread lock either), the three journal writes with the outcome still recorded, the remember effect with the replay kept, the explicit save, the manual memory, and lock conflicts (a head held by a writer neither blocks nor closes a custom start), a terminated closed-head read of the legacy start and a Thread with no head never reading as closed. Where a case claims lock order, a held ACCESS EXCLUSIVE lock on the migration ledger stops the strict observation, and the evidence is taken from the one backend then waiting for that lock: its statement must be the exact first statement of the observation, it must hold no row-level lock, it must belong to the pool of the entry under test, and the rows the entry locks afterwards must still be free, after controls showing that the probes and the lock counter see a real holder. This covers the three journal writes, the remember effect, the manual memory, and each of the three transactions of the explicit save, whose acknowledged transaction start is held back so that the ledger is locked only after the save passed its registration verification; for the start of IO and the registration it also shows the head untouched while the save waits for its operation row, after the quota row was taken. Verified on PostgreSQL 17.11: workspace formatting and five strict Clippy configurations clean; the eight cases passed in three consecutive runs on the tree merged with 038, and all eight fail against the unchanged consumers. Regression on the same instance and tree: the thread history writer (12), writer observation order (1) and admission (12) targets, the component provider context (1), the custom provider runtime (15) and run selection (7), the tool journal fence (18), commit (3), journey (6) and application (5) targets, the memory administration (3), current-authority (8), management-authority (4), provenance (5), recall authority (8) and recall baseline (8) targets, the run runtime (5), run effect receipt (8) and thread begin (6) targets, repositories (5), the artifact source run (2), MCP OAuth runtime (3), MCP protocol (11) and Google Drive runtime (31) targets passed, and 41 of 42 artifact administration unit cases passed. These fail identically on the unchanged base on this host and were not edited: the current-dataset custom runtime (nine of sixteen; the base fails the same nine and, on some runs, a tenth, timing-dependent start-budget case, which passed five of five here), the remember effect receipts (two of eighteen), the artifact registration integration (two of thirty), the artifact read lifecycle (three of three, no file-descriptor oracle tool), the positive restart recovery case, and one artifact read authority unit case. Two existing test files receive test-only compatibility fixes whose intent is preserved, because they drive the current production paths on a database that the strict observation now rejects: the memory provenance cases insert their fixture rows at the historical native prefix 37 and now upgrade to the current native prefix once those rows exist (the case that provisions prefix 36 and upgrades itself is untouched), and the tool journal fence damage case re-enables the Run triggers before its final missing-rows assertions, so the product calls run with the guard restored while the dropped checks stay dropped; both targets pass in full on the same instance (5 of 5 and 18 of 18). The strict observation costs about 270 ms per call in the unoptimized test profile on this host. Claimed paths (twelve): crates/openbot-infra/src/thread_history_writer.rs, crates/openbot-infra/src/provider/context.rs, crates/openbot-infra/src/provider/custom.rs, crates/openbot-infra/src/repo/tools/fence.rs, crates/openbot-infra/src/repo/tools.rs, crates/openbot-infra/src/memory_admin/remember_effect.rs, crates/openbot-infra/src/memory_admin.rs and crates/openbot-infra/src/artifact_administration.rs; the new crates/openbot-infra/tests/thread_history_consumer_closure.rs and the two adjusted fixtures crates/openbot-infra/tests/memory_provenance.rs and crates/openbot-infra/tests/tool_journal_fence/binding.rs; and this ledger. Accepted on the exact head b3dcce1 (tree 2712343): the independent non-author review returned GO with no P1 after three rounds (one fixed the explicit-save registration lock order and bound every lock-order claim to the entry's own backend, one approved the two fixture adjustments, and one removed a second complete writer-schema observation from the current-dataset custom start found by acceptance, where an existing headers-timeout case had started failing); the remaining notes are documentation-level or untested-classification items. Controller acceptance in a separate build directory on an owned PostgreSQL 17.11 instance found workspace formatting and five strict Clippy configurations clean, the eight cases passing in ten consecutive full runs (80/80), and a directed sweep of 33 test binaries (the 035-037 thread history targets, the 038 stop/clear target, context, custom provider runtime and run selection, current-dataset custom runtime, tool journal, memory, run runtime and effect receipts, thread begin, repositories, MCP, Google Drive and artifact source/current-read/registration/ read-lifecycle targets, and the artifact administration unit cases) matching unchanged main case by case; the current-dataset headers-timeout case fails intermittently on both (9/10 candidate, 13/15 main, same assertion). This entry records acceptance before merge; the merge is recorded separately. In-flight drain and the actual clearing of persisted copies (ACK2), a cross-member stop, byte deletion and Ready stay open; the built-in providers are covered by the context admission right before sampling, and the window between the post-read check and the provider send belongs to the drain.

- HANDOFF-20261013-CLOUD (cloud-session delivery and handoff record; does not occupy a V7-IMPL/V7-COMP/UI5-P* number): V7-IMPL-038 (single-thread stop/clear intent producer, ACK1) merged via [#199](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/199) as `788e532d5debf8eb70f785439da587ceab21fdf5` (accepted product head `5f6e3eb`; the merge head `c27f727` adds only the ledger record). V7-IMPL-039 (consumer-side closure: context load, both custom start routes, tool decision/retry/capability attach, remember effect, explicit save and manual memory with a source) merged via [#201](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/201) as `ad17d38fb09b58b8cec8943a599dfe2d260cbea9` (accepted head `b3dcce1`; the merge head `63663af` adds only the ledger record). Both were accepted in the Linux cloud environment on owned loopback PostgreSQL 17.11 servers built from the official source tag REL_17_11, because the package mirrors were unreachable there; owned test servers in that environment need TimeZone America/Los_Angeles, since the frozen public-schema oracle stores timestamp-with-time-zone constraint literals deparsed in that zone, and track_activity_query_size 16384 for the lock-wait probes. Pending local macOS verification: a Desktop Local window Host case for the 038 producer (needs macOS; not yet written) and the existing 034 Local case; in the cloud environment the two 034 management cases with 250/300 ms original deadlines fail identically on unchanged main. Not started because they need owner decisions first: who may stop another member's in-flight Run in a shared thread and how that cancel is recorded; what the actual clearing of persisted thread history covers and how it fits the existing storage constraints (message rows can only be deleted, memory and tool-call foreign keys, immutable reconciliation-required Runs, soft-deleted threads, where the completion fact is stored); run-output expiry with byte deletion; restore consistency; and artifact references on BeginThreadRun (semantics of the injected copies, deletion propagation, limits and host binding, and a Desktop mapping that needs macOS). ACK2 (in-flight Run drain and the actual clearing of persisted copies), Ready and the M0-M2 milestones remain open.

- RULINGS-20261013 (rulings handed over for adoption; does not occupy a V7-IMPL/V7-COMP/UI5-P* number): on 2026-10-13 the owner asked the cloud controller to rule, from the first source, on every item that HANDOFF-20261013-CLOUD listed as needing owner decisions (stopping other members' in-flight Runs, clearing persisted thread history and its storage constraints, run-output expiry with byte deletion, restore consistency, artifact references on BeginThreadRun) and on the registered residuals of V7-IMPL-038 and V7-IMPL-039. The rulings were handed over privately for adoption into the first source, which must precede their implementation; this ledger does not restate them. Planned order after adoption: V7-IMPL-040 stop and drain of a closed thread's in-flight Runs, V7-IMPL-041 clearing of persisted copies including run-output expiry, V7-IMPL-042 artifact references; restore consistency is not yet scheduled. ACK2, Ready and the M0-M2 milestones remain open, and the 038 Local window Host case and the existing 034 Local case remain pending local macOS verification.

- TESTFIX-20261013-05（仓库守卫配置，不占V7-IMPL/V7-COMP/UI5-P*编号）：精确白名单一处gitleaks误报。V7-IMPL-038（[#199](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/199)，合并`788e532d`，该行由`da1ae88`引入）的`crates/openbot-infra/src/thread_history_authority/stop_clear.rs`第164行（SQL常量`CLOSE_HEAD`里给`closure_actor_id`、`closure_auth_generation`、`closed_at`三列赋值的续行，行首4个空格，行尾为空格加反斜杠）被gitleaks默认规则`generic-api-key`报告；该行不含任何凭据字面值，规则以`q.auth_generation`里的`auth`为关键字、以其后的逗号为赋值运算符，把紧随其后的`closed_at`列赋值（捕获值的熵为3.59）当作了密钥。已提交的`.gitleaks.toml`（blob `1a8faf56`）没有对应白名单，所以凡使用已提交配置的环境（全新克隆、在worktree内直接运行`python3 tools/repository_guard.py staged`、手动CI）扫描main都会被拦下。本机未暴露的原因：git hooks经绝对`core.hooksPath`指向主检出的`.githooks`，运行的是主检出的`tools/repository_guard.py`，其`ROOT`取脚本所在仓库根，读取的是主检出工作树里一处尚未提交的同形精确白名单，故本机提交与推送均通过。修改：仅在`.gitleaks.toml`末尾追加一条`[[allowlists]]`（一个空行加7行，已有条目未动，结果blob `64362a69`，与主检出该未提交版本逐字节一致）——`targetRules=["generic-api-key"]`、`condition="AND"`、`regexTarget="line"`，`paths`锚定上述文件，`regexes`锚定该行的完整文本（含行首4个空格与行尾空格加反斜杠），与`crates/openbot-infra/tests/scoped_egress.rs`的既有精确行白名单同形。该行的逐字文本只以转义后的正则形式存在于此白名单中，本条目不逐字引用它，因为白名单按文件路径限定，未转义的同一文本出现在台账里会被同一规则再次报告。验证用该分支worktree自己的`tools/repository_guard.py`（`ROOT`即该worktree）：修改前（main内容）`staged`被拦（“Gitleaks rejected the candidate”，退出1），同配置的脱敏全树扫描（1414个文件）恰1条发现，`generic-api-key`位于`stop_clear.rs`第164行；修改后`staged`通过（“staged tree accepted (1414 files)”，退出0），脱敏扫描0条发现。负向对照均未提交，并以`git restore --source=HEAD --staged --worktree`还原后核对`stop_clear.rs`的HEAD、index、工作树blob同为`f27cdd5b`：(a) 在该文件另一处（第151行）临时插入形如`let api_key = "<40位随机hex>";`的假凭据，守卫仍拦截（退出1），脱敏扫描恰1条发现且位于被插入行，原SQL行（后移为第165行）仍被抑制，故白名单不绑定行号；(b) 在第164行本身追加一个假凭据，该行不再等于被白名单的精确文本，守卫拦截，发现位于第164行；(c) 将同一段SQL行以同名文件放入另一目录（仅在内存中扫描，未写入仓库），仍被报告，故白名单限定该文件路径。`git diff --check`无问题。仅守卫配置与台账：未改产品代码、SQL、迁移、第一真源或交接索引，未运行全量CI或手动Actions。合并前验收（[#206](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/206)，精确head `413ccb0`，tree `8218758`）：非作者独立复核结论GO，P1、P2为零；两条P3（白名单按整行文本匹配、不绑定行号；`paths`带`(?:^|/)`前缀）与三条既有精确行白名单同形，被抑制的只是这一固定且不含凭据的列赋值文本，登记不改。主控在该head的独立临时worktree中用其自己的守卫复验：`staged`通过（1414个文件）；`history`扫描该head可达的全部历史通过（890个提交、3627个文件版本）；未改动的main（`b79a51e`）用其自己的守卫`staged`被拦。负向对照均未提交，逐项还原并核对blob：在另一行插入假凭据、在被白名单的那一行行尾追加假凭据、把同一行放入另一路径的新文件，守卫均拦截，脱敏扫描分别定位到第151行、第164行与新文件第1行；正向对照在文件首行前插入一行、使该行移到第165行，守卫仍通过。

- TESTFIX-20261013-02（034 H09/H10 原始期限预算，测试专用，不占V7-IMPL/V7-COMP/UI5-P*编号）：openbot-server lib `auth::thread_history_management_tests`（11例，自有PG17.11隔离库，`--include-ignored`）在未改代码的main `b79a51e`上，本机失败形态为：串行（`--test-threads=1`）10轮中H10失败3/10（`deadline has elapsed`），默认并行3轮中H09 3/3失败（`real actor lock wait never reached`）、H10 2/3失败；云端Linux上这两例在main与当时的候选上均3/3失败、信息相同。同模式的Desktop H12（350ms）本机8/8通过，本项不改。根因：H09末段的300ms与H10被扣ACK一段的250ms是034测试自选的原始期限常数（6d154a5引入，此后未变）；034交付后，035（9bb74ec）在BEGIN前加入HQ admission schema核验，036（71161ab）在事务内`validate_thread_origin_in_transaction`加入完整writer schema核验，未优化测试profile下单次事务内完整观察约135–270ms，使observer在期限内到不了被测步骤：H09末段在schema观察阶段耗尽300ms，observer以Unavailable退出、从未到达actor锁，`wait_actor_lock`3秒后报never reached；H10在250ms内走不到真实ROLLBACK，`GuardedTransaction::rollback`发现期限已过即返回DeadlineExceeded而不发ROLLBACK（`pool.rs`第419–421行），proxy永远等不到，`hold.wait()`3秒超时。§4.2 R454第4项对期限只规定`min(original_deadline, entry_now+5s)`，由pool/schema/BEGIN/锁/语句/真实ACK共用、不分阶段续期，无亚秒级要求；036合同与R459第3项要求完整观察且在首个行锁之前。按第一真源裁决：**产品缺陷：无**，属测试期限过紧。修改（仅测试，`thread_history_management_tests.rs`的两行）：H09末段与H10该段的原始期限改为2秒（小于5秒上限，足以越过两段schema观察）；H09在`wait_actor_lock`证实真实等待后断言observer尚未结束，并在Unavailable类拒绝之后、writer回滚之前断言未续期（`Instant::now() <= original + 1s`；writer仍持锁，若按阶段续期会等到约entry+5s）；H10在`hold.wait()`后断言ROLLBACK在期限内真实到达proxy且observer尚未结束，并把固定300ms睡眠改为睡到`original + 200ms`，其后“late original ACK accepted”断言与`hold.release()`不变、次序不变，二者之间补与H09同口径的不续期断言（`Instant::now() <= original + 1s`；ROLLBACK ACK被扣住期间若期限续期到entry+5s，observer会等到约original+3s）。原意图保留：原始期限包含actor锁等待且不分阶段续期；被扣住、超过原始期限的ROLLBACK ACK不得放行成功。2秒预算在极端高负载下仍可能不够，此时失败表现为带明确信息的失败（如H09的`real actor lock wait never reached`或`original observer ended before the real actor lock wait`，H10的`deadline has elapsed`或`original ROLLBACK reached the proxy after the original deadline`），不会假通过，属环境判读。验证（自有PG17.11隔离库，独立CARGO_TARGET_DIR，以`grep -a`核对二进制含新断言文案而main二进制不含；机器为12核，同时有无关编译任务并行）：未改main二进制与H10追加不续期断言之前的候选在同机同期各跑一轮对照（各轮开始时的1分钟负载均值：候选7.8–20.7，main 4.5–15.4）：候选串行10轮、默认并行10轮每轮11例全过（各110/110），H09、H10各用`--exact`单独跑10次均过；main串行10轮失败2轮（均为H10 `deadline has elapsed`），并行10轮全部失败（每轮H09 `real actor lock wait never reached`与H10 `deadline has elapsed`同时失败），H09单跑失败1/10、H10单跑失败10/10。追加H10不续期断言后的最终版本在同一PG上从零重建并复跑（各轮开始时的1分钟负载均值9.0–21.0）：串行10轮、默认并行10轮每轮11例全过（各110/110），H09、H10各用`--exact`单独跑10次均过（10/10、10/10）。两处不续期断言另经本地负向对照（不提交）验证：让observer忽略调用方原始期限、改用entry+5s后，H09 3/3以“original deadline renewed during the actor lock wait”失败，H10 3/3以“original deadline renewed while the ROLLBACK ACK was held”失败，生产文件随即还原并核对blob一致，还原后重建的候选二进制与被测二进制的SHA-256前16位相同。rustfmt通过；`cargo clippy --locked -p openbot-server --lib --tests -- -D warnings`在main与候选上同样退出101，报同一组9个既有错误（`http/threads.rs:36`未使用的import一个；`gateway_callback_rendezvous_tests.rs`四处与`gateway_token_dispatch_tests.rs`一处的`format!`冗余引用；`gateway_registration_dispatch_tests.rs`的`cmp_owned`与`is_multiple_of`各一处；本文件第915行`AckProxy.control`字段的`type_complexity`），均不在本次改动的行，未改；追加H10断言后的最终版本重跑结果相同，无新增。认领路径两个：`crates/openbot-server/src/auth/thread_history_management_tests.rs`与本台账；无生产代码、迁移、依赖、Cargo.toml/Cargo.lock、其它测试文件及Desktop H12改动。未运行全量CI或手动Actions。合并前验收（[#205](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/205)，精确head `be3726a`，tree `a3ff42d`）：非作者独立复核两轮结论均为GO，P1、P2为零；首轮两条P3（H10扣ACK期间的不续期上界、2秒预算在极端负载下的失败形态）已在`be3726a`实施；次轮两条P3：其一，1秒容差确定能抓到的是续到entry+5s或按阶段给满5秒这类合同明确禁止的形态，再收紧会引入调度竞态，登记不改；其二，H09末段在writer持锁期间`task.await`没有上界，若生产锁等待失去期限，用例会挂起而不是失败，这是本PR之前即有的写法，已排入后续server测试卫生项改为有界等待；同形的Desktop - TESTFIX-20261013-01（038 Desktop Local window Host 用例，测试专用，不占V7-IMPL/V7-COMP/UI5-P*编号）：补上V7-IMPL-038单线程stop/clear意图producer（ACK1）在Desktop Local窗口宿主上的outer用例，即交接记录中“待macOS本机验证”的第一项。**产品缺陷：无；仅新增测试，无生产代码、迁移、依赖或Cargo.lock改动。** 新增`crates/openbot-desktop/src/tauri_host/thread_history_stop_clear_local_tests.rs`，内含单个`#[ignore]`用例`thread_history_stop_clear_real_local_window_closes_and_stale_window_refuses`（需`OPENBOT_TEST_PG_BIN`指向自有PostgreSQL17.11二进制目录）；入口是`crates/openbot-desktop/src/tauri_host.rs`文件末尾追加的一条`#[cfg(all(test, feature = "desktop-local-runtime", target_os = "macos"))]`的`#[path]`模块声明（4行，含一个空行）。夹具复制自034 Local用例（该文件未改动），改用自有名称（目录前缀、短路径socket目录名、bot与process名、回执标签），删去本用例用不到的helper，并新增本用例所需的断言与查询helper；夹具内容为：自有`create_new`临时app_root与socket目录、真实`initdb`/`pg_ctl`启动的PostgreSQL17.11 sidecar（先attestation，再以应用库连接）、真实owned installation、封存并经`verify_persisted`验证的Desktop vault canary、同一attested池上的原registry/authority/原issuer，窗口是真实`DesktopTauriProtocol`的`bind_window`/`unbind_window`；主密钥只是内存常量，不触Keychain、用户数据目录、TTY或受保护fixture，收尾由夹具停止并核验自有postmaster退出后删除自有目录。六段覆盖：(L0) 原窗口eligible，H为open、无intent；随后在开放的J上用新run id begin一个同J新Run，断言被接纳为新Run（回执的thread与run id一致、`replayed`为false），并立即把它走到终态（claim、确认、`Completed`，与夹具建首个Run共用同一个helper），确认J内恰2个Run、均为completed、无queued或running，这是L3“关闭后同J新Run被拒”的本地正向对照；之后才取H/Q（含行版本xmin）与原事实表的基线，并核整个sidecar恰一个open head、无intent；(L1) 同label解绑重绑得到新epoch后，旧窗口的调用在借用阶段被拒，内层原因断言恰为`Host(NotCurrent)`，H/Q（含行版本xmin）与原事实表不变，没有idle in transaction的会话；(L2) 另一事务持有actor行锁（该持锁事务同时作为idle in transaction计数能看见未结事务的正向对照），producer的原事务真实等在该锁上（`pg_stat_activity`查得Lock等待及其blocking pid），此时撤换窗口，锁释放后内层原因断言恰为`Host(NotCurrent)`，H/Q与原事实表不变、无遗留事务；(L3) 现窗口用L1被拒的同一request id关闭成功：确认为`CommitAcknowledged`，Debug不泄露operation id/J/request id，operation id为规范小写UUIDv7，独立SQL核得Q与H各一行，Q的intent_schema为1、intent_kind为stop_clear，operation/request/actor/generation与本次调用一致，anchor与thread_created_at与对应threads行一致，H为closed，closure的operation/request/actor/generation与`closed_at`五项分别等于Q的对应字段（`closed_at`对应Q的`recorded_at`），原事实表不变；核过Q/H后立即取H/Q含xmin的快照，其后同J新Run被拒`NotVisible`、034观察器仍Ok、同request id再调得`ExactReplayRollbackAcknowledged`且operation id相同，这三步每一步之后的H/Q含xmin都与该快照逐字节相同；(L4) 提交后再撤换窗口：旧窗口被拒（内层断言恰为`Host(NotCurrent)`）且H/Q不变，新窗口同request id得exact replay且operation id相同；(L5) 关闭protocol owner后，最后一个现窗口也被拒，内层断言恰为`Host(NotCurrent)`，H/Q不变；代码依据已写入测试注释：owner租约在protocol的Drop中同步关闭；排在“owner已关闭”检查之前的只有入口输入校验、剩余期限、scope、issuer已登记和binding存在这五项检查，本场景均不触发（输入合法、期限新取5秒、范围与角色相符且池未关、issuer已由`f.protocol()`登记、`fourth`带binding），所以第一项会拒绝的检查就是借用阶段对已关闭owner的`NotCurrent`，早于任何窗口表、身份来源或语句。本机macOS、自有PostgreSQL17.11、独立CARGO_TARGET_DIR下，加强后的用例连续5次全部通过（5/5，每次3.0至3.2秒），回执均为`THREAD_HISTORY_STOP_CLEAR id=L01 actual_actor_wait_pid=… stale_before_borrow_refused=true stale_in_tx_refused=true commit_ack=true exact_replay=true stale_after_commit_refused=true owner_closed_refused=true`，四处宿主拒绝（L1、L2、L4、L5）的内层原因都由断言恰为`NotCurrent`，不再只断言`Host`；同一二进制上034 Local用例`thread_history_management_real_local_canary_window_and_deadline_closure_are_required`运行1次，1/1通过；每次运行后未遗留自有postgres进程、临时app_root或socket目录。`cargo fmt --all --check`通过；`cargo clippy --locked -p openbot-desktop --lib --tests --features desktop-local-runtime -- -D warnings`因主线既有、本PR未改的5处告警失败（`artifact_read/lifecycle_tests.rs`的needless_borrow、`artifact_reads_tests.rs`的collapsible_if与type_complexity、`artifact_save_receipt_tests.rs`与`artifact_source_run_local_tests.rs`各一处large_enum_variant），仅放过这四类lint后退出0，新文件无任何告警（首个提交时已用临时探针确认新文件确在clippy范围内，探针已移除）。已知限制：Local sidecar池为attestation直连，没有ACK代理，不能扣住ROLLBACK/COMMIT的确认，“真实ACK之后才返回”在Local只有代码路径证据；扣住或丢失ACK的分类证据由Server用例（C06、C07）提供，本用例不转记。认领三路径：上述新文件、`crates/openbot-desktop/src/tauri_host.rs`（仅文件末尾追加）与本台账。未运行全量CI或手动Actions，未改第一真源或交接索引。合并前验收（[#204](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/204)，精确head `52c14d3`）：非作者独立复核两轮结论均为GO，P1、P2为零；首轮三条P3（四处宿主拒绝精确到`NotCurrent`；含xmin的H/Q快照在核过Q/H后立即取并逐步比对；L0开放J准入的正向对照）及台账措辞修正已在`ca8413b`实施，次轮一条P3（L5注释与本条对“owner已关闭”检查之前各项检查的列举不全）已在`50a1ab2`实施。主控在该head的独立worktree与独立CARGO_TARGET_DIR中复验（二进制含本用例名、回执标签与L0正向对照文案；`OPENBOT_TEST_PG_BIN`指向自有PostgreSQL17.11二进制）：本用例`--ignored openbot_contracts::command::BeginThreadRunBody;`（`unused_imports`，由a09efec引入）：删除该`cfg`与`use`两行及其后一个空行；该名在openbot-server整个crate（`src/`、`tests/`、`src/bin/`）中只出现在这一处，crate唯一的feature是`testkit`，`threads.rs`内除两处`cfg(test)`外没有feature门控，所以默认与`--features testkit`两种组合的测试构建都不使用它，生产构建不受影响；(2)至(5) `gateway_callback_rendezvous_tests.rs`第101、677、679、798行与(8) `gateway_token_dispatch_tests.rs`第105行（`useless_borrows_in_formatting`，共5处）：`format!`实参`&*x.state`（`state`是`Zeroizing<String>`）去掉多余的`&`，写作`*x.state`，`format!`本就按引用取参，输出不变；(6) `gateway_registration_dispatch_tests.rs`第141行（`cmp_owned`）：clippy建议把`raw.len().to_string()`换成`raw.len()`，但左侧是`serde_json::Value`，该用例的Python夹具把`content_length`记为JSON字符串（`str(len(data))`），换成`usize`即变成数值比较、字符串值永不相等，断言恒假，故不采纳，改为`sent[0]["content_length"].as_str() == Some(raw.len().to_string().as_str())`，仍是JSON字符串与十进制字符串逐字相等（与`Value == String`的原语义一致，写法与上一行`body_bytes`对称），并在该断言前加一行注释；(7) 同文件第315行（`manual_is_multiple_of`）：`nanosecond() % 1000 == 0`改为`nanosecond().is_multiple_of(1000)`（`u32`，除数非零，等价；`rust-version`为1.98.0，该方法已稳定）；(9) `thread_history_management_tests.rs`第915行（`type_complexity`）：`AckProxy.control`的类型`Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<Option<(oneshot::Sender<()>, oneshot::Receiver<()>)>>>`引入具名别名`type ProxyAckHold = (oneshot::Sender<()>, oneshot::Receiver<()>);`（代理侧两端：通知真实ROLLBACK ACK已到、等待放行），字段改为`Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<Option<ProxyAckHold>>>`，`AckProxy::start`里同类型的`None::<…>`也改用该别名。第10项（加强项，来自#205复核的P3-b）：H09末段（`thread_history_management_waits_recheck_current_facts_and_use_positive_nowait`的最后一段，原始期限为`Instant::now() + 2s`）在writer持锁期间的`task.await`没有上界，若生产的actor锁等待失去期限，用例会永久挂起而不是失败；改为`tokio::time::timeout_at(tokio::time::Instant::from_std(original + 1s), task)`有界等待，超时以“original deadline renewed during the actor lock wait”失败，JoinError仍`map_err`；其后的`unavailable(...)`分类与`Instant::now() <= original + 1s`断言保留不动。依据：§4.2 R454第4项对期限只规定`min(original_deadline, entry_now+5s)`，由pool/schema/BEGIN/锁/语句/真实ACK共用、不分阶段续期；H10的await由ACK proxy的8秒超时兜住，未改。验证（自有PG17.11，`server_version_num`为170011、TimeZone为America/Los_Angeles、`track_activity_query_size`为16kB，独立CARGO_TARGET_DIR，12核机器、验证期间1分钟负载约6至16）：`cargo fmt --all --check`退出0；clippy在未改的main上退出101、恰为上述9处，改后退出0，覆盖openbot-server的22个目标（lib、lib test、两个bin测试、18个集成测试）零诊断，`--features testkit`组合同样退出0。受影响测试逐名对照（共86例：`auth::thread_history_management_tests` 11、`auth::gateway_callback_rendezvous_tests` 16、`auth::gateway_registration_dispatch_tests` 18、`auth::gateway_token_dispatch_tests` 20、`http::threads` 21），改前（main `b3e0817`的lib测试二进制）与改后（候选二进制）各跑一次，四个auth模块用`--include-ignored --test-threads=1`，`http::threads`为普通单测（4线程）：改前改后“名称加状态”集合逐字一致，85例通过、1例失败；该1例是`gateway_callback_rendezvous_tests::r01_listener_before_any_create`，信息“bounded owned try_clone must reach original EMFILE24; another errno or no refusal earns no credit”，原因是它依赖启动器把子进程的文件描述符上限降到足以在512次克隆内触发EMFILE（测试源码注释所述），本机普通环境（软上限1048576）下改前改后同样失败，属环境前置，未改未修。gateway三个模块还需要环境变量`OPENBOT_TEST_TLS_PYTHON`指向带OpenSSL 3的Python，其TLS夹具使用Ed25519测试证书，系统自带的Python 3.9.6（LibreSSL 2.8.3）无法加载，本次用本机Homebrew的Python 3.12.14（OpenSSL 3.6.4），仅作为测试进程的环境输入。034的11例：串行（`--test-threads=1`）3轮、默认并行（12线程）3轮，每轮11/11通过（串行每轮43.2至44.4秒，并行每轮10.8至11.1秒）。第10项负向对照（本地，不提交）：把`crates/openbot-infra/src/thread_history_authority.rs`的`original_deadline.min(cap)`临时改为`cap`（生产放任锁等待到entry+5秒），重建后H09以`--exact --include-ignored`单跑3次，3/3以“original deadline renewed during the actor lock wait”失败且不挂起，失败点均在`original + 1001ms`（为取得该时间，本地另加一行临时`eprintln!`并随之还原），整例耗时5.77秒、5.46秒、5.47秒。补充对照（同样本地不提交）：把生产等待拉长到`entry + 20s`，新测试仍在`original + 1001ms`与`1002ms`失败（5.50秒、5.43秒），而改前写法的测试（HEAD版，仅加同样的计时输出）要到`original + 18002ms`才失败（22.45秒），即旧写法等待多久完全取决于生产的锁等待期限（writer在task结束前不会回滚），新写法有独立于生产的上界。还原：生产文件`git checkout`后blob与HEAD相同（`42a896f0`），测试文件还原后`git diff`的SHA-256与候选补丁相同，还原后重建的二进制与被测候选的SHA-256完全相同（前16位`125029febcf911d5`；改前main的二进制前16位为`71d4165bb0c31d80`）。关于`grep -a`：该断言文案在改前二进制中已存在（末尾的`check`），本项只是增加同一文案的第二处使用，相同字面量被编译器合并，改前改后的二进制中均只出现1次，所以`grep -a`不能区分新旧，区分依据是上述负向对照的失败点（`original + 1s`对`original + 18s`）。认领路径：`crates/openbot-server/src/http/threads.rs`（仅删`#[cfg(test)]`下未使用的import）、`crates/openbot-server/src/auth/gateway_callback_rendezvous_tests.rs`、`crates/openbot-server/src/auth/gateway_registration_dispatch_tests.rs`、`crates/openbot-server/src/auth/gateway_token_dispatch_tests.rs`、`crates/openbot-server/src/auth/thread_history_management_tests.rs`（仅别名与H09末段一处）与本台账；无生产代码、迁移、依赖、Cargo.toml/Cargo.lock改动。未运行全量CI或手动Actions。合并前验收（[#207](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/207)，精确head `f8f4f5b`，tree `1acba91`）：非作者独立复核结论GO，P1、P2为零；三条P3：第(1)项删除行数的措辞已在本条更正；“22个目标”经主控以`--message-format=json`实测核实，默认组合即为lib、lib test、两个bin测试与18个集成测试；依据句为条款转述，沿TESTFIX-20261013-02先例。主控在该head的独立worktree与独立CARGO_TARGET_DIR中复验（自有PostgreSQL17.11，`server_version_num`为170011、TimeZone为America/Los_Angeles、`track_activity_query_size`为16kB；gateway用例的TLS夹具使用带OpenSSL的Python 3.12）：`cargo fmt --all --check`通过；`cargo clippy --locked -p openbot-server --lib --tests -- -D warnings`及加`--features testkit`均退出0、零诊断；034的11例串行3轮（33/33）与默认并行10轮（110/110）全过，H09以`--exact`单跑10次10/10，各轮开始时1分钟负载5.8至13.6；`auth::gateway_registration_dispatch_tests`18/18、`auth::gateway_token_dispatch_tests`20/20、`http::threads`单测21/21；`auth::gateway_callback_rendezvous_tests`15过1败，失败的`r01_listener_before_any_create`需由启动器调低该子进程的文件描述符软上限（本机默认1048576，用例在512次克隆内到不了EMFILE），以`ulimit -n 128`与`ulimit -n 256`分别单跑均1/1通过，属环境条件，与本PR无关。

- TESTFIX-20261013-03（openbot-desktop测试卫生，测试专用，不占V7-IMPL/V7-COMP/UI5-P*编号）：**产品缺陷：无；仅改测试，无生产代码、迁移、依赖或Cargo.lock改动。** 三项。（一）command_audit既有失败：`tauri_host::tests::command_audit_list_and_generated_handler_are_exactly_joined`在main `f13ed00`上失败，信息为`left: 0 right: 2`（断言`#[tauri::command]`计数为2，实得0）。根因：该测试以`source.split("#[cfg(test)]").next()`截取“生产部分”，即第一个`#[cfg(test)]`之前的文本；`tauri_host.rs`在第一个`#[tauri::command]`（第3731行）之前有两条单行的测试专用`use`——第37–38行的`BeginThreadRunBody`（`a09efec`于2026-10-08引入）与第90–91行的`UiPreferences`（`365358e`于2026-10-03引入），所以只截到前36行，计数为0；真正的测试模块头（`#[cfg(test)]`换行`mod tests {`）在第4318–4319行，`window_chrome.rs`的在第171–172行。这两条`use`仍被测试使用，未删除。修改：两处截取都改为`split_once("\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {")`，找不到模块头时`expect`失败，不回退到整文件；断言值2与1不变。本地负向对照（不提交）：把两处模式分别改成不存在的`mod tests_gone {`，审计各自在“tauri_host.rs must keep its test module header”“window_chrome.rs must keep its test module header”处失败，文件随即还原并核对与受测版本逐字节一致。（二）5处strict Clippy既有错误：`cargo clippy --locked -p openbot-desktop --lib --tests --features desktop-local-runtime -- -D warnings`在`f13ed00`上退出101，恰5处，类别与文件同TESTFIX-20261013-01所记，行号即下列各处；逐条修复，不用`#[allow]`：`artifact_read/lifecycle_tests.rs:1051` needless_borrow，`real_terminal_state(&state)`改为`real_terminal_state(state)`（`state`已是引用）；`artifact_reads_tests.rs:835` collapsible_if，嵌套的`if let Some(diagnostic) = setup_diagnostic`与`if let Err(error) = &receipt`合并为let链，语句体整体左移一级，语句与次序不变（`git diff -w`只显示头部由2行变3行、删去一个收尾花括号）；`artifact_reads_tests.rs:2089` type_complexity，`cleanup_arm_local_object_fact`的返回类型改用同文件已有的具名别名`PhysicalLocalObjectFact`（同为七元组`(u64, u64, u32, u32, u64, u64, String)`，该函数的返回值本来就传给以该别名为参数类型的`PhysicalLocalPhaseGate::new`），未新增别名；`artifact_save_receipt_tests.rs:914`的`HeldReceipt`与`artifact_source_run_local_tests.rs:1033`的`HeldSource` large_enum_variant，`Application`变体的载荷改为`Box<Result<AppReply, AppError>>`，构造处加`Box::new`，匹配处`matches!(*result, …)`，两个枚举只在单个任务的返回值里传递，行为不变（两用例的`last_owner_drop`各变体走的就是这条`Application`路径，修改后其回执行集合与修改前一致）。（三）034 Desktop H12末段（`thread_history_management_local_tests.rs`，“原始期限包含actor锁等待且不分阶段续期”那一段）与TESTFIX-20261013-02修正前的Server H09同形：350ms是测试自选常数；035在BEGIN前加入HQ admission schema核验、036在事务内加入完整writer schema核验之后，observer必须在期限内越过这两段观察才到得了actor锁，期限过紧时以`actual Local actor wait was not reached`失败；该段又只断言结果落在Unavailable、DeadlineExceeded、RollbackUnproven、RollbackAcknowledgedAfterDeadline之一，没有断言“不续期”，也没有给`task.await`设上界——若生产把期限续到entry+5s，用例仍会通过（只是变慢），回执却打印`original_deadline_not_renewed=true`；这一点已实测：旧版H12（350ms、无不续期断言）在下述续期变体上3/3通过，每次约9.1–9.3秒（未改动的生产约5.9秒）。依据：§4.2 R454第4项（期限上限、全程共用、不分阶段续期，无亚秒级要求）。本机未改动的H12单次通过（5.85秒），故此处不是修一个失败，而是去掉对350ms的依赖并补上不续期断言。修改（仅该文件末段两行，其余步骤、helper与期限常量不变）：原始期限改为2秒；`wait_actor`证实真实等待后断言observer尚未结束（“original Local observer ended before the real actor lock wait”）；`task.await`改为`tokio::time::timeout_at`到`original + 1s`的有界等待，超时以“original Local deadline renewed during the actor lock wait”失败；原结果变体断言保留；其后、`tx.rollback()`之前补`Instant::now() <= original + 1s`断言（同一信息）。2秒预算在极端高负载下仍可能不够，此时失败表现为带明确信息的失败（“actual Local actor wait was not reached”或“original Local observer ended before the real actor lock wait”；生产实现正确而调度停顿超过1秒时也可能出现“renewed”信息），不会假通过，须结合负载判读。本地负向对照（不提交）：把`crates/openbot-infra/src/thread_history_authority.rs`的`original_deadline.min(cap)`临时改为`cap`（调用方原始期限被忽略、改用entry+5s），重建后新版H12 3/3以“original Local deadline renewed during the actor lock wait”失败，每次约6.8–6.9秒，不挂起，无遗留进程或目录（旧版H12在同一变体上3/3通过，见上）；随即`git checkout`还原该生产文件，核对其blob与HEAD一致，还原后重建的二进制与被测二进制的SHA-256前16位相同。验证（本机macOS、Rust 1.98.0、自有PostgreSQL 17测试前缀，独立CARGO_TARGET_DIR，修改前后同一目录增量构建；机器上另有无关构建并行，下列负载为各次开始时的1分钟负载均值）：`cargo fmt --all --check`退出0；strict clippy修改后退出0，零lint诊断（只有build script链接阶段`rust-objcopy`因找不到libLLVM而失败的环境警告，修改前同样出现，非lint）。`tauri_host::`全部非ignored用例（`--lib --features desktop-local-runtime`，不带`--include-ignored`）：修改前73过1败63 ignored，失败的是command_audit；修改后74过0败63 ignored；全部逐名状态行对照，唯一差异是该用例由FAILED变ok；`--list`所列468个用例名前后逐字相同。四个受影响文件内的15例全部是`#[ignore]`用例，没有非ignored用例；先读夹具再运行：它们都以内存里的`MemorySecretStore`注入`OsSecretStore`，在`std::env::temp_dir()`下自建0700属主根目录，PostgreSQL来自`OPENBOT_TEST_PG_BIN`指向的自有二进制，所有子进程stdin置空且只调用`pg_config`、对自身PID的`lsof`与`kill -0`，不触Keychain、用户数据目录或真实TTY，故修改前后各以`--ignored --exact`逐个运行这15例，加038 Local用例共16例（均不带`--include-ignored`或`--ignored`的整体过滤）：修改前15过1败（负载5.8–22.3），失败的是`artifact_reads::tests::actual_local_original_window_is_required_through_physical_cleanup_completion`，在夹具创建阶段报“genuine Prepared Local physical setup: dependency_unavailable - CHECKPOINT-20261013-LOCAL (local macOS controller session checkpoint; does not occupy a V7-IMPL/V7-COMP/UI5-P* number): on 2026-10-13 the rulings handed over as RULINGS-20261013 were adopted privately into the first source, and the local macOS verification and test hygiene were delivered as test-only PRs. Each was accepted on its exact head (independent non-author review GO with no P1 or P2 outstanding; controller acceptance in a separate worktree and build directory on an owned PostgreSQL 17.11 instance) and merged at that head: TESTFIX-20261013-01 (038 Desktop Local window Host case) via [#204](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/204) as `f13ed00d92c9a9dc44c4fea16792cc35a8ed6947`; TESTFIX-20261013-02 (034 H09/H10 original deadline budgets with non-renewal assertions) via [#205](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/205) as `b3e0817600aceaeb66b9049ad831624d553cc8a7`; TESTFIX-20261013-05 (exact gitleaks allowlist for a verified false positive) via [#206](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/206) as `2c7841813fb416c6b6b4f9f168f11b0740ca461f`; TESTFIX-20261013-04 (server test strict Clippy cleanup and a bounded H09 lock wait) via [#207](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/207) as `1551c0675712b6e909fc2d2020cac546a1ba9f27`; TESTFIX-20261013-03 (desktop command audit split, desktop test strict Clippy cleanup, H12 deadline budget with a non-renewal assertion) via [#208](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/208) as `009c42445d0eaf5e486ce7d0d88b602b518e5f97`; TESTFIX-20261013-06 (infra test strict Clippy cleanup and the SSO test feature gate) via [#209](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/209) as `0f41147cfbe7f9b42eec13e115426fdcca92576f`. Strict Clippy with `-D warnings` is now clean on main for openbot-server (`--lib --tests`, default and `testkit`), openbot-desktop (`--lib --tests`, `desktop-local-runtime`) and openbot-infra (`--lib --tests` default and `server-runtime`, and the `desktop-local-vault` lib); the 034 H01-H12 cases and the 038 Local case pass on this host, so neither Local case remains pending local verification. Open at this checkpoint: V7-IMPL-040 (system drain of a closed thread's in-flight runs, no migration) is published as [#210](https://github.com/acosmi/wrokbot/pull/210) at head `330aaac`; the independent review of its product head `ea481c4` returned GO with one P2 (the user cancellation producer and the remember effect must apply the consumer's closed-history check to a system acceptance row) and five P3, all addressed by the repair commits `d182ec7` and `330aaac`, which still need the incremental independent review, controller acceptance on the exact head and a merge of main into the branch (the ledger is the only conflicting file) before the exact-head merge; its two newest commits carry a different Co-Authored-By model name from the earlier ones. TESTFIX-20261013-07 is not yet published: on main, `tauri_host::artifact_reads::tests::actual_local_original_window_is_required_through_physical_cleanup_completion` fails intermittently with `dependency_unavailable dependency=artifacts` because its fixture saves while the source run's write transaction still holds the run row, and the save's NOWAIT source locks refuse at once, which is contract behavior (product defect: none); the fix makes the Local artifact fixtures wait for a terminal source run before their original save. Next: V7-IMPL-041 (clearing of persisted copies, native_0052) after 040 merges; V7-IMPL-042 (artifact references) has been scouted and needs a first-source clarification of its claimed paths and of the statement shape of its in-transaction recheck, which waits for the owner's confirmation before dispatch. Environment notes: the callback `r01_listener_before_any_create` case needs its launcher's low file-descriptor limit (it passes under `ulimit -n 128` and `ulimit -n 256`); one transient Local sidecar start failure (`helper_journal_invalid`) in an artifact lifecycle case did not reproduce in eight alternating reruns. ACK2, Ready and the M0-M2 milestones remain open.

- V7-IMPL-040: Adds the trusted internal system drain of a closed Thread history's in-flight Runs to the existing `PostgresThreadHistoryAuthority` (`drain_closed_history_runs_before`), the stop that follows the acknowledged 038 stop/clear intent. It takes a Thread id and the original deadline (the call's deadline is the earlier of that and five seconds from entry, shared by every pool checkout, statement and terminal acknowledgement) and carries no Auth context, Host binding or registry: like the cancellation claim, the dispatch claim and the lease recovery it is a system action, bound only to the authority's pool, deployment and tenant and to the closed head and its intent read from the database. A malformed Thread id is InvalidInput; a head that is open or absent, has no intent or belongs to another deployment or tenant is NotVisible. For each queued or running Run of the Thread it writes that Run's one durable cancellation acceptance, `{runId, threadId, source: thread_history_closed, operationId}` with the intent's operation id and no requester, in the outbox columns, class, destination and sequence of the user's producer, and notifies the same control channel; it never grants any user the right to cancel another member's Run. Each Run has its own short guarded READ COMMITTED transaction: a lock wait limit equal to the time left of the shared deadline (never above five seconds), a lock-free read of the head and the intent, `FOR UPDATE NOWAIT` on the Run row alone, then a new lock-free statement that reads the Run's existing acceptance by identifier and by delivery key; only when none exists does it insert, notify and COMMIT, and every other result rolls back and waits for its real acknowledgement. No existing acceptance row is ever locked, so the consumer's acceptance-then-Run order gets no reverse edge and no new wait. A Run that is gone or already terminal when its row is locked is skipped, so whoever committed a terminal first keeps it (a worker's own, the lease recovery's, the closed-history context refusal's); a pending or delivering acceptance of either lawful shape counts as already requested and is neither replaced nor rewritten, so a user's own request stays the user's; a queued row, a delivered or dead-lettered acceptance on a Run that still runs, another identifier on the delivery key, a foreign requester, another operation and any other shape are Corrupt, so a resume never loops. A refused NOWAIT lock (a custom v2 start that still holds its shared Run lock included), a deadlock, a serialization failure, a lock wait that ran out, a deadline that runs out inside a statement and every other database failure roll the current Run back and return Unavailable, without retry and with the Runs committed before it kept; a deadline that runs out in the transaction owner itself, at its begin, commit or rollback, is the existing DeadlineExceeded, CommitAcknowledgedAfterDeadline or RollbackAcknowledgedAfterDeadline result and an unacknowledged COMMIT is CommitUnknown; none is a Conflict or a closed answer. The error type of the 038 producer is reused without a new variant. The typed result (non-Serde, non-Clone, no public constructor, redacted Debug) reports the operation id, the Runs written now, the Runs already requested and the Runs still queued or running after a last lock-free count; the drain is complete when that count is zero. The three readers of a Run's cancellation acceptance share one shape judgement and one closure question in the run runtime. The shape is exactly the user shape `{runId, threadId, requestedBy}` or the system shape, each naming that Run and Thread, and nothing else. The question is whether the Thread's head is closed by exactly the operation the system row names, with that intent belonging to the Run's Thread and head, and head, intent and Thread sharing a deployment and tenant; all three readers build it with one function, so it is the same lock-free predicate. The cancellation consumer compares a requester only for the user shape; for the system shape it asks the question after the run lock and dead-letters a row the head and intent do not bear out as a scope binding, so a forged system row cannot stop a Run of an open or differently closed Thread, and a payload that carries a system key without being the lawful system shape as a broken binding, keeping a missing requester for a payload with no system key; an accepted Run then ends through the existing unstarted or signalled path as cancelled with the fixed terminal payload and no body. The user's cancellation producer answers an existing pending or delivering system acceptance as already requested only when the question, asked in the same statement that reads the row, is answered yes; a system-shaped row the head and intent do not bear out is Corrupt like every other foreign shape, and a user other than the Run's actor, the creator and an admin included, is still NotVisible. The remember effect treats the system acceptance as a valid acceptance like the user's under the same condition, asked in the single lock-free statement that follows the run lock: a fresh effect is refused and the exact replay of a committed receipt is returned, while an unbound system row is a corrupt binding for both. No writer schema proof is taken because nothing of Thread history is written. No migration (native 0052 stays free), SQL, schema fixture, dependency, audit event, public command, route, DTO, capability id, UI, relay startup scan, tombstone, clearing or deletion. Nine real-database cases passed on an owned loopback PostgreSQL 17.11 instance, each alone ten times (90 of 90), as three consecutive whole-file rounds (3 of 3) and once with a single test thread: another member's in-flight Run, which had streamed a chunk while the history was open, gets after the real ACK1 exactly the four-key acceptance with the intent's operation id and no requester without needing the creator's Session or access, wakes the control channel (and no later step does), and ends cancelled with the fixed terminal payload and no assistant message while another Thread's Run is untouched; the creator and an admin member stay NotVisible before and after, the Run's own member observes AlreadyRequested with the system row untouched, a user's earlier request is counted as already requested and kept, and five forged rows (a system row naming an operation that did not close its Thread, one naming another Thread's operation, one on a Thread whose head is open, a payload with the source key alone and a payload with neither requester nor system key) are each Corrupt for the user's producer with every row left as it was, and are dead-lettered by the consumer as a scope binding three times, a broken binding once and a missing requester once, with their Runs left running; reruns and four concurrent callers write exactly one acceptance with no changed row version, and a Run that never started is stopped without ever being dispatched; a contended second Run of the same Thread (a fixture, since production begins one foreground Run per Thread) ends the call as Unavailable with the first Run's acceptance kept, and the resume completes after consumption; the shared lock of a simulated custom v2 start window is Unavailable until released; a real custom v2 start, whose provider handshake a local listener never answers, holds its original transaction and shared Run lock in its send window, so the drain is Unavailable with nothing written, the start returns within its own three second budget, and the drain then writes and the relay revokes exactly the accepted Runs and dispatches none, so the Run ends cancelled without a body within six seconds of the resumed drain; a signalled streaming child cannot stream on (its chunk is refused) and stops cancelled without a body; completed, failed, cancelled and reconciliation-required Runs, a Run the lease recovery ended first and a Run whose worker completed after the acceptance all keep their terminals unchanged; four kinds of held Run lock are refused at once, a server-detected deadlock is Unavailable and a wait that ran out of budget is Unavailable or the transaction owner's DeadlineExceeded, none writes anything, and the same call then resumes; a remember effect's exact replay returns its committed receipt while the system acceptance is pending and after it is delivered and a fresh effect is a Conflict, while a system row naming another operation or another Thread's operation is a corrupt binding for the exact replay and the fresh effect alike and the lawful row is read as lawful again, and on an open Thread a system-shaped row and four broken shapes are Corrupt; an open head, a Thread without a head and an unknown Thread are NotVisible and malformed ids InvalidInput, the same Thread turns visible exactly when ACK1 closes it, a queued row is Corrupt while the earlier Run keeps its acceptance, and eight corrupt acceptance shapes are Corrupt and never rewritten. Three unit tests pin the shape judgement and the dead-letter classification. Temporary one-line mutations on the final tree each broke exactly the intended cases and were restored byte for byte: the consumer rejecting the system shape (seven cases), the consumer skipping the closure question (the forged-row case), the user producer or the remember effect skipping the question (one case each) or rejecting the lawful system row (one case each), the former dead-letter code classification (the forged-row case and its unit test), a waiting instead of NOWAIT Run lock (three cases), no notification (one), overwriting an existing acceptance (four) and cancelling a queued row (one). Formatting is clean, and the four strict Clippy configurations (`openbot-infra` lib and tests with default features and with only `server-runtime`, `openbot-server` tests, `openbot-infra` lib with `desktop-local-vault`) report exactly the diagnostics that unchanged main at f13ed00 reports in the same configuration, by target and by message (30, 32, 6 and 0 distinct; 9 errors in the server lib tests and none in the Desktop-only configuration), all in files this task does not touch, so none is new. A directed sweep on the merged tree, each target built from its own tree just before its run: the consumer closure target 8 of 8, run runtime 5 of 5, custom model v2 runtime 16 of 16, the three unit cases of the shape judgement and the dead-letter classification 3 of 3, the server thread HTTP cases 21 of 21 and the agent runtime target's end-to-end user cancel case 1 of 1; run cancellation (its one case reports thread_target_not_visible) and remember effect receipts (16 of 18; the other two need an owned restart controller and a PostgreSQL binary directory) fail identically on unchanged main at f13ed00 built from a separate export (the candidate's run cancellation binary contains the consumer's new head-and-intent statement, main's does not). The 034 module (`auth::thread_history_management_tests`, 11 cases, whose deadline cases now carry 2 s original deadlines) passed 3 of 3 serial and 3 of 3 default-parallel rounds on the merged tree. Residuals: the 40001 class is mapped structurally but cannot be produced, because each Run's transaction is READ COMMITTED; the lock wait limit equals the time left of the shared deadline, so the client-side deadline decides first and an over-budget wait ends as Unavailable inside a statement or as the transaction owner's DeadlineExceeded, while the server-side limit only bounds a wait that outlives a dropped client; the real custom v2 start is held at its provider handshake by a local listener that never answers, so a provider that answers and streams while the history closes was not exercised; the first Unavailable or Corrupt Run ends the call, so Runs after it wait for the next call; there is no production caller yet, the intended callers being the clearing producer (V7-IMPL-041) and a future restore producer; ACK2 clearing of persisted copies, byte deletion, restore consistency and Ready stay open. Claimed paths (seven): crates/openbot-infra/src/thread_history_authority/run_drain.rs (new), crates/openbot-infra/src/thread_history_authority.rs (module declaration and re-export only), crates/openbot-infra/src/run_runtime.rs, crates/openbot-infra/src/thread_directory.rs, crates/openbot-infra/src/memory_admin/remember_effect.rs, the new crates/openbot-server/tests/thread_history_run_drain_postgres.rs, and this ledger.
